# Re:Zero — relationship continuity ledger

This file is the sole maintained relationship owner in the synchronized V09 transaction, effective after its complete publication gate. Existing source-facing freezes remain unchanged; master §6 routes here rather than maintaining a competing full dyad table.

## 0. Responsibility and promotion boundary

V09 earns the registered relationship-continuity responsibility. Newly mutual repair, erased recognition of a living person, unavailable counterpart response and an incompatible internal-family future require independently retrievable histories and terms. A single latest affinity or one undifferentiated dyad state cannot represent these changes safely. This is a qualitative promotion at a source-verified arc boundary, not a claim that another count of characters warrants another file.

Necessary earlier history migrates explicitly retrospectively from master §6 at base commit `a5f762b00fbd1bb45517c833346c3f5f8f8bfc88` and the unchanged V01–V08 freezes. All twenty-five former dyad/network responsibilities retain evidence routes below. New V09 relations and transitions are added, and master §6 is routing only. This ledger did not exist prospectively during earlier readings.

Ownership is divided by question:

| question | sole maintained responsibility |
|---|---|
| When did this occur, and what was discarded or carried forward? | `REZERO_EVENT_STATE_AND_ROUTE_LEDGER.md` |
| Who knows a proposition, how, and who did not receive it? | `REZERO_KNOWLEDGE_AND_INFORMATION_ASYMMETRY_LEDGER.md` |
| What histories and terms are mutually enacted, privately remembered, refused, imposed or repaired between these people? | this ledger |
| What formal power, patronage, resources or institutional rules constrain their choice? | `REZERO_INSTITUTIONS_FACTIONS_AND_POWER_LEDGER.md` |
| What causal mechanism is established, hypothetical, costly or countered? | `REZERO_MECHANICS_METAPHYSICS_AND_WORLD_MODEL_LEDGER.md` |
| What person-level phase, ordinary evidence, voice or reconstruction readiness follows? | master character, ordinary-life, terminology and readiness sections |

Relationship rows cite information and institutional conditions where needed; they do not reproduce complete holder, chronology, causal or resource tables. Frozen VNN arguments are historical evidence, not rival maintained current models.

Historical entering selection paragraph — exact V14-era wording retained, not current default:

The source horizon is V14. E018 continues alive at Subaru's actual Beatrice-library entry,17 deaths/18 segments. Emilia FIRST PASSES/SECOND STARTS; Garfiel actually reaches outside and both site fights only begin. Select actual recipients: private trial history is not Subaru's knowledge, old E017 intimacy is not current mutual memory, and Ram/Otto's confidentiality differs from reader withholding. No second/third result, accepted Beatrice departure, completed Rem/village extraction, barrier release, wager or Arc4 end is supplied. V15 remains sealed through V14 publication and the earned separate Emilia V01–V14 construction/review gate; both older model/review scopes remain unchanged.

For each relationship select:

1. Subaru's retained history, if he is a participant;
2. the counterpart's actually demonstrated history or present inability to respond;
3. current mutually enacted conduct;
4. reader-only discarded, private or route-local evidence;
5. practical power, consent, dependency, obligation, affection, fear, rivalry and address relevant to the decision;
6. the unresolved condition that prevents a stronger claim.

Subaru's retained bond is not the counterpart's retained bond. Counterpart gratitude is not consent to every plan. A formal alliance is not personal intimacy; a friend address need not erase hurt; care need not be romantic reciprocation. A person's being forgotten does not make helping that person impossible for someone newly informed.

Rem remains breathing/unresponsive for over a year at the new household, after actual V15 extraction; Subaru talks nightly and changes flower water daily. Recalled Emilia/Beatrice support is not both physically present at this visit. Ram's sister memories remain missing. Select an actual pre-ambush phase for Rem speech; no current reply, own-memory test, exclusive marriage/children or waking consent is invented. Carmilla's likeness and internal escape-family remain different selected premises.

Stable dyad IDs are `RZ-R-NNN`. Transition IDs in §3 are `RZ-RT-NNN`. These are analytical retrieval labels, not fictional offices or new source claims. `VNN Cxxx` routes to that freeze's stable claim; `VNN §n` routes to its source-facing argument and exact loci.

## 4. Reconstruction and refusal safeguards

V15 retains actual earlier Beatrice refusals before her selected hand; the new bond does not validate every intrusion. Emilia's gladness and new finger promise do not furnish old informed kiss assent/apology or an origin telling. A visible vow can sanction without preventing Roswaal's private threatened violence. Petra's nonforgiveness and outside civilian nonreceipt defeat universal pardon. Composite domination is reader-only, not proof every prior Shima attachment was false.

Use R001's received repair without assuming it was already present at the V04 rupture. Use R011's friend address without treating the beating as erased. Use R002's current grief without fabricating an answer from Rem. Use R026's present choice without requiring restored autobiographical intimacy. Use R029 only under its flight premise, not as the current family's hidden biography.

When asked to infer an unobserved response, select the actual supported phase and material recipient, information, status and resource coordinates. If those cannot be supplied, record `RELATIONSHIP_STATE_UNKNOWN`, `KNOWLEDGE_STATE_UNKNOWN`, `ONLY_DISCARDED_STATE_EVIDENCE`, `ONLY_ALTERNATE_ROUTE_EVIDENCE` or another protocol abstention as appropriate. The internal-fragment transfer limit is applicable even though its container is `MAIN_LN`; that does not reclassify the whole volume as an external alternate witness.

Readiness stays in master and the character protocol. The ledger does not promote persons, claim a full marriage from a narrated courtship, equate affection with a contract, or treat generated unfamiliar-situation probes as new evidence. A future relationship specialist must explain what independent work it adds beyond these maintained selections.

V11 requires setting-local and holder-local care. Use R019's actually received E015 friendship without granting a later Otto that memory. Use R004's bodily kinship without inventing recovered sisterhood. Use R033's real listening without making Echidna's account omniscient or the relief a permanent cure. Use R043–045 separately: childlike affection, restorative anger and an argumentative bridge have different causal and moral consequences. Final absence is not an observed person's hidden assent or a complete fate census.

V12 further requires representation-local and consent-local selection. Beatrice's request for help is not assent to Subaru's ready solution; her protective death is not erasure of her mixed wishes. Current Rem does not speak through Carmilla. Constructed Julius/Wilhelm/Ferris grief is not new mutual history. A pressured copied person obeying is not a volunteer, and later freedom promised does not undo the earlier expenditure. Roswaal's fatal use of Ram is not authorized by affection or a soul pledge. Echidna's true benefit sentences do not disclose every relevant path cost. Refusing her formal offer is not already rescuing Beatrice or cancelling previous limited terms.

V13 further requires act-local and recipient-local selection. Otto's current friend address/partial briefing is actual and does not give him RBD/tea or E015 memories. Emilia's stated support is not substitution; until-morning company is a specific promise, and receiving a loving wall message leaves apology owed. The one-second kiss warning/eye closure has explicit uncertainty, not unqualified enthusiastic consent or an invented verbal refusal. Garfiel's proxy sister is not actual Frederica's present testimony; his conditional commander address is not every belief surrendered. Alma rejects imposed pity; Ram's hidden cooperation does not erase Roswaal devotion; Puck's new aid does not undo unilateral cancellation. Patrasche's current grudge and refused touch are not erased-loop revenge or human romance. These conditions are part of positive change, not excuses to declare either total cure or no growth.

V18 further requires listener-local, patient-local and responsibility-local selection. Fredo/sister's observed warmth is positive local-life evidence, not an all-household census or Garfiel's received reunion. Julius's responsibility question, Otto's resistance and the received apology remain actual without becoming codified law or a perfect-knight grade. Crusch's refusal constrains treatment even when Subaru's private later plan persists; new bodily survival does not erase the earlier cost. Priscilla's Schult care, Al-directed violence, pride pressure and self-staked trust remain recipient-specific, not a universal benevolence/cruelty/immunity formula. Emilia's wish that Fortuna/Geuse had married does not establish that they did or that a mutual wedding had been planned. Select historical waking Mimi/Beatrice only with the actual supported phase; no current new reply, assent, body cure or source/grade extension follows. Ram chronology remainsOPEN; C116/M344's full feline speech/healing subject remains Frederica, not Garfiel. Earlier maintenance work orders below are dated historical records, not pending dependencies to repeat.

V19 further requires event-local permission and private-holder selection. Priscilla permits Liliana to fasten Priscilla's dress; the imposed kiss is a different direction/act and useful effect does not supply consent. Emilia's correctable trust can be spoken to the wives without Subaru hearing it. Geuse address is neither first-ever utterance nor a whole revived reply. Liliana's declined caravan marriage invitation differs from a counterfactual return-home family; her earlier paid-tip amulet predates later song-as-gift insight. Kiritaka's retrieval permits rest, not sleeping interference or an invented waking/transport. Regulus's private wish is that death not please Emilia, ironically granted through nonrecall without erasing forest causality. V19 narration named the visible Anastasia body; V20 now distinguishes that body/current crisis operator from the awake human principal. Absent/sleeping people do not answer by proxy, and unfixed earlier meeting timing is not silently reassigned.
